class op4_loop_body:
    var_ranges = {p0: 8, p1: 768}
    index0 = 768*p0 + p1
    index1 = p0
    index2 = 1024
    def body(self, ops):
        get_index = self.get_index('index0')
        load = ops.load('primals_1', get_index)
        get_index_1 = self.get_index('index1')
        get_index_2 = self.get_index('index2')
        check_bounds = ops.check_bounds(get_index_1, get_index_2, False, False)
        get_index_3 = self.get_index('index0')
        load_1 = ops.load('primals_4', get_index_3)
        add = ops.add(load, load_1)
        get_index_4 = self.get_index('index1')
        load_2 = ops.load('buf1', get_index_4)
        sub = ops.sub(add, load_2)
        get_index_5 = self.get_index('index1')
        load_3 = ops.load('buf2', get_index_5)
        constant = ops.constant(768.0, torch.float32)
        truediv = ops.truediv(load_3, constant)
        constant_1 = ops.constant(1e-05, torch.float32)
        add_1 = ops.add(truediv, constant_1)
        rsqrt = ops.rsqrt(add_1)
        mul = ops.mul(sub, rsqrt)
        get_index_6 = self.get_index('index0')
        store = ops.store('buf4', get_index_6, mul, None)
        return store

        index_expr = ops.index_expr(get_index_2, torch.int64)

        to_dtype_1 = ops.to_dtype(sub, torch.bool, src_dtype = torch.float32)
        get_index_2 = self.get_index('index1')
        load_2 = ops.load('primals_3', get_index_2)
        to_dtype_2 = ops.to_dtype(load_2, torch.float32, src_dtype = torch.int64)
        constant_1 = ops.constant(1.0, torch.float32)
        sub_1 = ops.sub(constant_1, to_dtype_2)
        constant_2 = ops.constant(-3.4028234663852886e+38, torch.float32)
        where = ops.where(to_dtype_1, constant_2, sub_1)
        get_index_3 = self.get_index('index1')
        load_3 = ops.load('primals_3', get_index_3)
        to_dtype_3 = ops.to_dtype(load_3, torch.float32, src_dtype = torch.int64)
        constant_3 = ops.constant(1.0, torch.float32)
        sub_2 = ops.sub(constant_3, to_dtype_3)
        mul = ops.mul(where, sub_2)
        add = ops.add(load, mul)
        get_index_4 = self.get_index('index2')
        load_4 = ops.load('buf67', get_index_4)
        sub_3 = ops.sub(add, load_4)
        exp = ops.exp(sub_3)
        get_index_5 = self.get_index('index2')
        load_5 = ops.load('buf68', get_index_5)
        truediv = ops.truediv(exp, load_5)
        get_index_6 = self.get_index('index0')
        store = ops.store('buf69', get_index_6, truediv, None)
        return store
